# SVC metrics at 5 Mbit/s — the `r5000` sitting (2026-11-04, in progress)

What was run: the pre-registered SVC metrics protocol on one more link, 5 Mbit/s, for the three DBT volumes `dbts_a1`, `dbts_b1` and `dbts_c1`, at 1× and 4× CPU, all 13 variants, 1 of 10 whole rounds so far.
What it shows: at 5 Mbit/s the layered variants put a first picture up in about a tenth of a second and a base on every slice in under a second, while HTJ2K's first exact slice takes seconds and its last about two minutes; the layered variants' time to exact stays near the shipped payload's, except where `p3-*` sends middles twice (tables 1, 2a, 4, 7).
What it rests on: three public DBT volumes only (`dbts_a1` 56 × 1142×1785 12-bit, `dbts_b1` 50 × 2560×3328 10-bit, `dbts_c1` 69 × 976×2513 12-bit), one 4-vCPU host and headless Chromium 153.0.8010.12; it claims nothing for other series, modalities, hosts or browsers.
What is left: the remaining rounds of this link if not 10; the links `r50000`, `r20000` with 2 % loss and with ±20 ms jitter; the breast ultrasound cine and ABUS rows; part 5's rule, which another session applies once every condition is in.

**Terms.** *SVC*, scalable video coding: one AV1 stream in layers, a small lossy **base** (a quarter-size picture), in `p3-*` a full-size lossy **middle**, and the **exact** (lossless) top. *Layer-major*: every slice's base is sent first, then every middle, then every exact frame. *`k2`*: the shipped single-layer AV1 payload; *`htj2k`*: HTJ2K, exact; *`res`*: HTJ2K's half-size prefix first; *`a2-*`*: a base sent beside `k2`; *`p2-*`*, *`p3-*`*: two or three layers with the exact top predicted from the layer below; *`lossy-*`*: the lossy layers alone, nothing exact. *t_P* / *t_ℓ,all*: seconds from the fill's issue until every slice has a picture at layer ℓ or better; *t_X*: until every slice is exact. *`VOID`*: a visit dropped because the relay's own timing broke the rig's 1 ms bound (p99). *A/A*: `htj2k-aa`, the same files as `htj2k` again, whose spread is the resolution below which no difference is reported. The full protocol is [`docs/av1/README.md`](../../../../../docs/av1/README.md) §The scalable preview as a tool, 10; how each number is made, [`README.md`](README.md) §SVC metrics.

**Method**, the first sitting's ([`svc-results.md`](svc-results.md)) with only the link changed: the same harness, builds and commands, rebuilt in a fresh container from the pinned sources (the rebuilt `svc-shapes.tsv`, every shape's bytes, exact-frame count and layer error, is byte-identical to the committed one), `--links r5000` (5 Mbit/s, 20 ms each way) in place of `none,r20000`, a fresh browser a visit, cells and variants in a Williams order (`lab/order.mjs`), each `run.mjs` process's temporary directory removed after its round. The offline metrics (3, the layer errors, and 5's offline decode) do not depend on the link and are the first sitting's; this file repeats none of them, and part 2's model below reads that sitting's `svc-decode.json`. No harness change was needed.

**Host.** The same kind as the first sitting's: 4 vCPU Xeon at 2.1 GHz, 15 GB, browser and server on three cores, the relay alone on the fourth. **At 5 Mbit/s the host does not saturate: every exact fill is link-bound**, at 1× and at 4×. The numbers, recomputed from the raw rows below at every push, over every visit with an exact layer, `VOID` ones included: wire bits ÷ the last byte's time; the last exact frame against the last byte; the median t_X at 4× ÷ at 1× per series and variant.

* wire rate of the exact fills: 4.91 Mbit/s median, 4.87–4.91, over 66 fills
* last exact frame after the last byte: 0.24 s median, 0.02–1.69 s
* t_X at 4× ÷ at 1×: 1.000–1.009 over 33 series × variants
* `VOID`: 21 of 66 fills with an exact layer, 4 of 12 lossy-only; relay p99 0.81 ms median, max 5.16

More visits are `VOID` here than at 20 Mbit/s (where 2–8 of 130 a cell were): the relay's p99 self-timing went over the 1 ms bound on about a third of the visits, on lossy-only fills as on full ones. Why is *unverified*; that it is not CPU load is *derived* from 4× moving no fill's time beyond the figure above. Each cell reports its n, and the cells' n is what this sitting can claim.

**Exactness**, over every visit, `VOID` ones included, per cell in the list at the end of the tables: every exact frame against its checksum, every base and middle against native dav1d at its operating point, previews after their exact picture (`late`), lossy layers posted as exact (`stray`), and stall-flushes (a unit at the worker's 10 000 ms bound). The checks are the first sitting's, each mutated there ([`svc-mutations.jsonl`](svc-mutations.jsonl)); this sitting adds no check and so mutates none. **Totals: 3 850/3 850 exact frames equal to their checksums; 3 150 bases and 1 400 middles equal to native dav1d; 0 late  0 stray  0 stall-flushes.**
